'Create' : 'Edit' }}</div>\n7:         <div class=\"page-title\">{{ isNew ? (isDuplicateDraft ? 'New project (copy)' : 'New project') : 'Edit Project Settings' }}</div>\n8:         <div class=\"page-subtitle\">\n9:           {{ isDuplicateDraft\n10:             ? 'Review the copied settings, then save to create the project. Cancel discards the copy.'\n11:             : 'Define the decision, options, and optional factors before running comparisons.' }}\n12:         </div>\n13:       </div>\n14:       <v-btn variant=\"text\" @click=\"navigateTo(isNew ? '/projects' : `/projects/${projectId}`)\">Cancel</v-btn>\n15:     </div>\n16: \n17:     <v-tabs v-model=\"tab\" color=\"primary\" class=\"mb-4\">\n18:       <v-tab\n19:         v-if=\"!isNew\"\n20:         value=\"back-overview\"\n21:         :to=\"`/projects/${projectId}`\"\n22:       >\n23:         <i class=\"fa-solid fa-arrow-left mr-2\" /> Overview\n24:       </v-tab>\n25:       <v-tab value=\"overview\">Project</v-tab>\n26:       <v-tab value=\"options\">Options</v-tab>\n27:       <v-tab value=\"factors\">Factors</v-tab>\n28:       <v-tab value=\"review\">Review</v-tab>\n29:     </v-tabs>\n30: \n31:     <v-window v-model=\"tab\">\n32:       <v-window-item value=\"overview\">\n33:         <v-card class=\"glass-card\">\n34:           <v-card-text class=\"pa-6\">\n35:             <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap justify-space-between ga-2 mb-4\">\n36:               <div class=\"text-body-2 text-medium-emphasis\">Project settings and optional starter content from a template.</div>\n37:               <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap ga-2\">\n38:                 <v-btn size=\"small\" variant=\"tonal\" @click=\"openImportDialog('projects')\">\n39:                   <i class=\"fa-solid fa-clone mr-2\" /> Start with Template\n40:                 </v-btn>\n41:                 <v-btn\n42:                   size=\"small\"\n43:                   variant=\"text\"\n44:                   :disabled=\"isNew\"\n45:                   :title=\"isNew ? 'Save the project before creating a template' : undefined\"\n46:                   @click=\"openSaveAsTemplate\"\n47:                 >\n48:                   <i class=\"fa-solid fa-floppy-disk mr-2\" /> Save as Template\n49:                 </v-btn>\n50:               </div>\n51:             </div>\n52:             <v-row>\n53:               <v-col cols=\"12\" md=\"6\">\n54:                 <v-text-field v-model=\"form.project_title\" label=\"Project name\" />\n55:               </v-col>\n56:               <v-col cols=\"12\" md=\"6\">\n57:                 <v-text-field v-model=\"form.project_tag\" label=\"Short tag (unique)\" :disabled=\"!isNew\" class=\"mono\" hint=\"Used as a stable identifier\" persistent-hint />\n58:               </v-col>\n59:               <v-col cols=\"12\">\n60:                 <v-textarea v-model=\"form.project_description\" label=\"Description\" rows=\"3\" />\n61:               </v-col>\n62:               <v-col v-if=\"!isNew\" cols=\"12\">\n63:                 <div class=\"text-subtitle-2 mb-2\">Project branding image</div>\n64:                 <div class=\"text-body-2 text-medium-emphasis mb-3\">\n65:                   Optional image shown next to the project name on shared pages (upload up to 8 MB; stored near 1 MB).\n66:                 </div>\n67:                 <div class=\"d-flex align-center ga-4 flex-wrap\">\n68:                   <div class=\"project-branding-preview\">\n69:                     <img v-if=\"projectBrandingPreview\" :src=\"projectBrandingPreview\" alt=\"\" class=\"project-branding-preview__img\" />\n70:                     <div v-else class=\"project-branding-preview__empty\"><i class=\"fa-regular fa-image\" /></div>\n71:                   </div>\n72:                   <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap ga-2\">\n73:                     <input\n74:                       ref=\"projectBrandFileInput\"\n75:                       type=\"file\"\n76:                       accept=\"image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp,image/gif,.png,.jpg,.jpeg,.webp,.gif\"\n77:                       class=\"d-none\"\n78:                       @change=\"onProjectBrandFile\"\n79:                     />\n80:                     <v-btn size=\"small\" color=\"primary\" variant=\"tonal\" :loading=\"projectBrandUploading\" :disabled=\"!isAdmin\" @click=\"projectBrandFileInput?.click()\">\n81:                       {{ projectBrandHasImage ? 'Replace image' : 'Upload image' }}\n82:                     </v-btn>\n83:                     <v-btn v-if=\"projectBrandHasImage\" size=\"small\" variant=\"text\" color=\"error\" :loading=\"projectBrandDeleting\" :disabled=\"!isAdmin\" @click=\"onDeleteProjectBrand\">\n84:                       Delete\n85:                     </v-btn>\n86:                   </div>\n87:                 </div>\n88:                 <div v-if=\"projectBrandError\" class=\"text-caption text-error mt-2\">{{ projectBrandError }}</div>\n89:               </v-col>\n90:               <v-col cols=\"12\" md=\"6\">\n91:                 <div class=\"text-subtitle-2 mb-2\">Decision mode</div>\n92:                 <v-btn-toggle v-model=\"rankingMode\" mandatory color=\"primary\" divided class=\"flex-wrap\">\n93:                   <v-btn value=\"find_best\">Find Best</v-btn>\n94:                   <v-btn value=\"find_top_3\" :disabled=\"activeOptionCount < 6\">Find Top 3</v-btn>\n95: class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis mt-2\">\n99:                   {{ rankingModeHint }}\n100:                 </div>\n101:               </v-col>\n102:               <v-col cols=\"12\" md=\"6\">\n103:                 <v-switch v-model=\"form.disabled\" label=\"Close input (block new comparisons)\" color=\"warning\" />\n104:               </v-col>\n105:               <v-col cols=\"12\" md=\"6\">\n106:                 <div class=\"text-subtitle-2 mb-1\">Comparison close date</div>\n107:                 <div class=\"d-flex align-center ga-2 flex-wrap\">\n108:                   <v-menu v-model=\"endTimeMenu\" :close-on-content-click=\"false\">\n109:                     <template #activator=\"{ props: menuProps }\">\n110:                       <span\n111:                         class=\"text-body-1\"\n112:                         style=\"cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline;text-underline-offset:3px\"\n113:                         v-bind=\"menuProps\"\n114:                       >{{ endTimeDisplay }}</span>\n115:                     </template>\n116:                     <v-card>\n117:                       <v-date-picker\n118:                />\n123:                       <v-card-actions>\n124:                         <v-btn variant=\"text\" @click=\"clearEndTime\">Clear</v-btn>\n125:                         <v-spacer />\n126:                         <v-btn variant=\"text\" @click=\"endTimeMenu = false\">Done</v-btn>\n127:                       </v-card-actions>\n128:                     </v-card>\n129:                   </v-menu>\n130:                   <v-btn size=\"small\" variant=\"text\" @click=\"endTimeMenu = true\">Change</v-btn>\n131:                   <v-btn v-if=\"form.end_time\" size=\"small\" variant=\"text\" @click=\"clearEndTime\">Clear</v-btn>\n132:                 </div>\n133:                 <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis mt-1\">\n134:                   Optional. Collection closes at 11:59 PM on the selected date. None means no deadline.\n135:                 </div>\n136:               </v-col>\n137:               <v-col cols=\"12\" md=\"6\">\n138:                 <v-switch\n139:                   v-model=\"form.private_participation\"\n140:                   label=\"Private participation (hide individual names in results)\"\n141:                   color=\"primary\"\n142:                   :disabled=\"privateParticipationSwitchDisabled\"\n143:                   :hint=\"privateParticipationHint\"\n144:                   persistent-hint\n145:                 />\n146:               </v-col>\n147:               <v-col cols=\"12\">\n148:                 <div class=\"text-subtitle-2 mb-2\">Balance influence</div>\n149:                 <div class=\"text-body-2 text-medium-emphasis mb-3\">\n150:                   How group Results weigh comparisons when participants contribute different amounts.\n151:                 </div>\n152:                 <v-btn-toggle\n153:                   v-model=\"form.participant_influence_mode\"\n154:                   mandatory\n155:                   color=\"primary\"\n156:                   divided\n157:                   class=\"flex-wrap\"\n158:                 >\n159:                   <v-btn value=\"comparisons\">Comparisons equal</v-btn>\n160:                   <v-btn value=\"balanced\">Balanced</v-btn>\n161:                   <v-btn value=\"participants_normalized\">Participants normalized</v-btn>\n162:                 </v-btn-toggle>\n163:                 <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis mt-2\">\n164:                   {{ influenceModeHint }}\n165:                 </div>\n166:               </v-col>\n167:               <v-col cols=\"12\" md=\"6\">\n168:                 <v-text-field\n169:                   v-model.number=\"form.min_expected_passes\"\n170:                   type=\"number\"\n171:                   min=\"1\"\n172:                   max=\"5\"\n173:                   step=\"1\"\n174:                   label=\"Minimum expected passes\"\n175:                   hint=\"Target passes per participant (1–5). Used for progress and encouragement.\"\n176:                   persistent-hint\n177:                   density=\"compact\"\n178:                 />\n179:               </v-col>\n180:               <v-col cols=\"12\" md=\"6\">\n181:                 <v-text-field\n182:                   v-model.number=\"form.max_recommended_passes\"\n183:                   type=\"number\"\n184:                   :min=\"maxPassesFloor\"\n185:                   max=\"10\"\n186:                   step=\"1\"\n187:                   label=\"Maximum recommended passes\"\n188:                   hint=\"Recommended ceiling (≥ minimum expected, ≤ 10). Hard stop is this value + 1.\"\n189:                   persistent-hint\n190:                   density=\"compact\"\n191:                 />\n192:               </v-col>\n193:               <v-col v-if=\"aiFeaturesEnabled\" cols=\"12\">\n194:                 <div class=\"text-subtitle-2 mb-1\">AI agents</div>\n195:                 <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis mb-3\">\n196:                   Each selected model votes as its own AI participant through the minimum expected number of groups.\n197:                   Names appear as AI AGENT: model. The same model cannot vote twice.\n198:                   When Include AI Agents is off, the list is kept but not queued.\n199:                 </div>\n200:                 <div class=\"d-flex align-start ga-4 flex-wrap\">\n201:                   <v-switch\n202:                     v-model=\"form.include_ai_agents\"\n203:                     label=\"Include AI Agents\"\n204:                     color=\"primary\"\n205:                     hide-details\n206:                     density=\"compact\"\n207:                     class=\"flex-grow-0 mt-1\"\n208:                     style=\"flex:0 0 auto\"\n209:                   />\n210:                   <v-select\n211:                     v-model=\"form.ai_voter_models\"\n212:                     :items=\"aiVoterItems\"\n213:                     item-title=\"title\"\n214:                     item-value=\"value\"\n215:                     label=\"AI participants\"\n216:                     multiple\n217:                     chips\n218:                     closable-chips\n219:                     density=\"compact\"\n220:                     class=\"flex-grow-1\"\n221:                     style=\"min-width:220px\"\n222:                     :loading=\"loadingAiModels\"\n223:                     :hint=\"aiVoterHint\"\n224:                     persistent-hint\n225:                   />\n226:                 </div>\n227:               </v-col>\n228:               <v-col cols=\"12\">\n229:                 <v-btn\n230:                   variant=\"text\"\n231:                   size=\"small\"\n232:                   class=\"px-0\"\n233:                   @click=\"advancedOpen = !advancedOpen\"\n234:                 >\n235:                   <i :class=\"advancedOpen ? 'fa-solid fa-chevron-down mr-2' : 'fa-solid fa-chevron-right mr-2'\" />\n236:                   Advanced settings\n237:                 </v-btn>\n238:                 <v-expand-transition>\n239:                   <div v-show=\"advancedOpen\" class=\"mt-3 pa-4 rounded-lg\" style=\"background: rgba(var(--v-theme-on-surface), 0.03)\">\n240:                     <div class=\"text-subtitle-2 mb-1\">Factor importance spread</div>\n241:                     <div class=\"text-body-2 text-medium-emphasis mb-3\">\n242:                       How strongly ranked factors differ in weight when blending overall results. Not saved to templates.\n243:                     </div>\n244:                     <div class=\"d-flex align-center ga-3 mb-1\">\n245:                       <span class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\" style=\"min-width: 7.5rem\">Factors are more equal</span>\n246:                       <v-slider\n247:                         v-model=\"form.factor_weight_floor_alpha\"\n248:                         :min=\"0.1\"\n249:                         :max=\"0.9\"\n250:                         :step=\"0.05\"\n251:                         reverse\n252:                         color=\"primary\"\n253:                         thumb-label\n254:                         hide-details\n255:                         class=\"flex-grow-1\"\n256:                       />\n257:                       <span class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\" style=\"min-width: 8.5rem; text-align: right\">Factors are more spread out</span>\n258:                     </div>\n259:                     <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis mb-5\">\n260:                       Default is the middle position. Lower values emphasize top-ranked factors more.\n261:                     </div>\n262:                     <v-text-field\n263:                       v-model.number=\"form.participant_influence_min_comparisons\"\n264:                       type=\"number\"\n265:                       min=\"3\"\n266:                       step=\"1\"\n267:                       label=\"Minimum comparisons before amplifying influence\"\n268:                       hint=\"Soft floor (3+). Participants below this are not over-amplified.\"\n269:                       persistent-hint\n270:                       density=\"compact\"\n271:                       style=\"max-width: 22rem\"\n272:                     />\n273:                     <div class=\"text-subtitle-2 mb-1 mt-6\">Questions per group</div>\n274:                     <div class=\"text-body-2 text-medium-emphasis mb-3\">\n275:                       How many pairwise comparisons each group asks. The Ford–Johnson estimate for the current item count is the guide; you can raise or lower it within the allowed range.\n276:                     </div>\n277:                     <v-text-field\n278:                       v-model.number=\"form.option_questions_per_group\"\n279:                       type=\"number\"\n280:                       :min=\"optionQuestionBounds.min || 1\"\n281: :hint=\"optionQuestionHint\"\n285:                       persistent-hint\n286:                       density=\"compact\"\n287:                       :disabled=\"optionQuestionBounds.max <= 0\"\n288: <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap justify-space-between ga-2 mb-4\">\n340:               <div class=\"text-body-2 text-medium-emphasis\">At least two named options are required. Disabled options are excluded from comparisons.</div>\n341:               <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap ga-2\">\n342:                 <v-btn size=\"small\" variant=\"tonal\" @click=\"addOption\">\n343:                   <i class=\"fa-solid fa-plus mr-2\" /> Add option\n344:                 </v-btn>\n345:                 <v-btn size=\"small\" variant=\"text\" @click=\"openImportDialog('options')\">\n346:                   <i class=\"fa-solid fa-clone mr-2\" /> Import from Template\n347:                 </v-btn>\n348:                 <v-btn\n349:                   size=\"small\"\n350:                   variant=\"text\"\n351:                   :disabled=\"isNew\"\n352:                   :title=\"isNew ? mr-2\" /> Save as Template\n356:                 </v-btn>\n357:               </div>\n358:             </div>\n359:             <div v-for=\"(opt, idx) in options\" :key=\"idx\" class=\"rank-row mb-3\">\n360:               <div class=\"d-flex ga-3 align-start\">\n361:                 <div class=\"rank-number\">{{ idx + 1 }}</div>\n362:                 <div class=\"flex-grow-1\">\n363:                   <v-text-field v-model=\"opt.title\" label=\"Option title\" density=\"compact\" hide-details class=\"mb-2\" />\n364:                   <v-textarea v-model=\"opt.description\" behind the ranking.\n386:             </v-alert>\n387:             <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap justify-space-between ga-2 mb-2\">\n388:               <v-radio-group v-model=\"factorMode\" inline class=\"ma-0\">\n389:                 <v-radio label=\"Overall only\" value=\"overall\" />\n390:                 <v-radio label=\"Multiple factors\" value=\"multi\" />\n391:               </v-radio-group>\n392:               <v-btn\n393:                 size=\"small\"\n394:                 variant=\"text\"\n395:                 :disabled=\"isNew\"\n396:                 :title=\"isNew ? 'Save the project before creating a template' : undefined\"\n397:                 @click=\"openSaveAsTemplate\"\n398:               >\n399:                 <i class=\"fa-solid fa-floppy-disk mr-2\" /> Save as Template\n400:               </v-btn>\n401:             </div>\n402: \n403:             <div v-if=\"aiFeaturesEnabled && factorMode === 'overall'\" class=\"mb-4\">\n404:               <v-btn\n405:                 size=\"small\"\n406:                 variant=\"tonal\"\n407:                 color=\"primary\"\n408:                 :disabled=\"!canSuggestFactors || suggestingFactors\"\n409:                 :loading=\"suggestingFactors\"\n410:                 :title=\"canSuggestFactors ? undefined : 'Add a project title and description first'\"\n411:                 @click=\"suggestFactorsFromProblem\"\n412:               >\n413:                 <i class=\"fa-solid fa-wand-magic-sparkles mr-2\" /> Suggest factors\n414:               </v-btn>\n415:             </div>\n416:             <template v-if=\"factorMode === 'multi'\">\n417:               <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap ga-2 mb-4\">\n418:                 <v-btn size=\"small\" variant=\"tonal\" @click=\"addFactor\">\n419:                   <i class=\"fa-solid fa-plus mr-2\" /> Add factor\n420:                 </v-btn>\n421:                 <v-btn size=\"small\" variant=\"text\" @click=\"openImportDialog('factors')\">\n422:                   <i class=\"fa-solid fa-clone mr-2\" /> Import from Template\n423:                 </v-btn>\n424:                 <v-btn\n425:                   v-if=\"aiFeaturesEnabled\"\n426:                   size=\"small\"\n427:                   variant=\"tonal\"\n428:                   color=\"primary\"\n429:                   :disabled=\"!canSuggestFactors || suggestingFactors\"\n430:                   :loading=\"suggestingFactors\"\n431:                   :title=\"canSuggestFactors ? undefined : 'Add a project title and description first'\"\n432:                   @click=\"suggestFactorsFromProblem\"\n433:                 >\n434:                   <i class=\"fa-solid fa-wand-magic-sparkles mr-2\" /> Suggest factors\n435:                 </v-btn>\n436:               </div>\n437:               <div v-for=\"(c, idx) in criteria\" :key=\"idx\" class=\"rank-row mb-3\">\n438:                 <div class=\"d-flex ga-3 align-start\">\n439:                   <div class=\"flex-grow-1\">\n440:                     <v-text-field v-model=\"c.title\" label=\"Factor title\" density=\"compact\" hide-details class=\"mb-2\" />\n441:                     <v-textarea v-model=\"c.description\" label=\"Description\" rows=\"2\" density=\"compact\" hide-details class=\"mb-2\" />\n442:                     <v-text-field\n443:                       v-model=\"c.comparison_question\"\n444:                       label=\"Comparison question\"\n445:                       placeholder=\"Which option has lower Engineering Cost?\"\n446:                       hint=\"Optional. label=\"Factor comparisons per group\"\n                      :hint=\"factorQuestionHint\"\n                      persistent-hint\n                      density=\"compact\"\n          <template #append-inner>\n                        <v-chip v-if=\"!factorGroupSizeExplicit\" size=\"x-small\" variant=\"tonal\">Default</v-chip>\n                        <v-btn\n                          v-else\n                          size=\"x-small\"\n                          variant=\"text\"\n                          class=\"px-1\"\n                          @click.stop=\"resetFactorGroupSize\"\n                        >Reset</v-btn>\n                      </template>\n                    </v-text-field>\n                  </div>\n                </v-expand-transition>\n              </v-col>\n            </v-row>\n          </v-card-text>\n        </v-card>\n      </v-window-item>\n\n      <v-window-item value=\"options\">\n        <v-card class=\"glass-card\">\n          <v-card-text class=\"pa-6\">\n            <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap justify-space-between ga-2 mb-4\">\n              <div class=\"text-body-2 text-medium-emphasis\">At least two named options are required. Disabled options are excluded from comparisons.</div>\n              <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap ga-2\">\n                <v-btn size=\"small\" variant=\"tonal\" @click=\"addOption\">\n                  <i class=\"fa-solid fa-plus mr-2\" /> Add option\n                </v-btn>\n                <v-btn size=\"small\" variant=\"text\" @click=\"openImportDialog('options')\">\n                  <i class=\"fa-solid fa-clone mr-2\" /> Import from Template\n                </v-btn>\n                <v-btn\n                  size=\"small\"\n                  variant=\"text\"\n                  :disabled=\"isNew\"\n                  :title=\"isNew ? 'Save the project before creating a template' : undefined\"\n                  @click=\"openSaveAsTemplate\"\n                >\n                  <i class=\"fa-solid fa-floppy-disk mr-2\" /> Save as Template\n                </v-btn>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n            <div v-for=\"(opt, idx) in options\" :key=\"idx\" class=\"rank-row mb-3\">\n              <div class=\"d-flex ga-3 align-start\">\n                <div class=\"rank-number\">{{ idx + 1 }}</div>\n                <div class=\"flex-grow-1\">\n                  <v-text-field v-model=\"opt.title\" label=\"Option title\" density=\"compact\" hide-details class=\"mb-2\" />\n                  <v-textarea v-model=\"opt.description\" label=\"Description\" rows=\"2\" density=\"compact\" hide-details class=\"mb-2\" />\n                  <v-checkbox v-model=\"opt.disabled\" label=\"Disabled (exclude from comparisons)\" density=\"compact\" hide-details color=\"warning\" />\n                </div>\n                <v-btn icon variant=\"text\" :disabled=\"options.length <= 2\" @click=\"options.splice(idx, 1)\">\n                  <i class=\"fa-solid fa-trash\" />\n                </v-btn>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n            <div class=\"d-flex justify-end mt-2\">\n              <v-btn size=\"small\" variant=\"tonal\" @click=\"addOption\">\n                <i class=\"fa-solid fa-plus mr-2\" /> Add option\n              </v-btn>\n            </div>\n          </v-card-text>\n        </v-card>\n      </v-window-item>\n\n      <v-window-item value=\"factors\">\n        <v-card class=\"glass-card\">\n          <v-card-text class=\"pa-6\">\n            <v-alert type=\"info\" variant=\"tonal\" class=\"mb-4\">\n              Factors show what matters when people compare options — the “why” behind the ranking.\n            </v-alert>\n            <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap justify-space-between ga-2 mb-2\">\n              <v-radio-group v-model=\"factorMode\" inline class=\"ma-0\">\n                <v-radio label=\"Overall only\" value=\"overall\" />\n                <v-radio label=\"Multiple factors\" value=\"multi\" />\n              </v-radio-group>\n              <v-btn\n                size=\"small\"\n                variant=\"text\"\n                :disabled=\"isNew\"\n                :title=\"isNew ? 'Save the project before creating a template' : undefined\"\n                @click=\"openSaveAsTemplate\"\n              >\n                <i class=\"fa-solid fa-floppy-disk mr-2\" /> Save as Template\n              </v-btn>\n            </div>\n\n            <div v-if=\"aiFeaturesEnabled && factorMode === 'overall'\" class=\"mb-4\">\n              <v-btn\n                size=\"small\"\n                variant=\"tonal\"\n                color=\"primary\"\n                :disabled=\"!canSuggestFactors || suggestingFactors\"\n                :loading=\"suggestingFactors\"\n                :title=\"canSuggestFactors ? undefined : 'Add a project title and description first'\"\n                @click=\"suggestFactorsFromProblem\"\n              >\n                <i class=\"fa-solid fa-wand-magic-sparkles mr-2\" /> Suggest factors\n              </v-btn>\n            </div>\n            <template v-if=\"factorMode === 'multi'\">\n              <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap ga-2 mb-4\">\n                <v-btn size=\"small\" variant=\"tonal\" @click=\"addFactor\">\n                  <i class=\"fa-solid fa-plus mr-2\" /> Add factor\n                </v-btn>\n                <v-btn size=\"small\" variant=\"text\" @click=\"openImportDialog('factors')\">\n                  <i class=\"fa-solid fa-clone mr-2\" /> Import from Template\n                </v-btn>\n                <v-btn\n                  v-if=\"aiFeaturesEnabled\"\n                  size=\"small\"\n                  variant=\"tonal\"\n                  color=\"primary\"\n                  :disabled=\"!canSuggestFactors || suggestingFactors\"\n                  :loading=\"suggestingFactors\"\n                  :title=\"canSuggestFactors ? undefined : 'Add a project title and description first'\"\n                  @click=\"suggestFactorsFromProblem\"\n                >\n                  <i class=\"fa-solid fa-wand-magic-sparkles mr-2\" /> Suggest factors\n                </v-btn>\n              </div>\n              <div v-for=\"(c, idx) in criteria\" :key=\"idx\" class=\"rank-row mb-3\">\n                <div class=\"d-flex ga-3 align-start\">\n                  <div class=\"flex-grow-1\">\n                    <v-text-field v-model=\"c.title\" label=\"Factor title\" density=\"compact\" hide-details class=\"mb-2\" />\n                    <v-textarea v-model=\"c.description\" label=\"Description\" rows=\"2\" density=\"compact\" hide-details class=\"mb-2\" />\n                    <v-text-field\n                      v-model=\"c.comparison_question\"\n                      label=\"Comparison question\"\n                      placeholder=\"Which option has lower Engineering Cost?\"\n                      hint=\"Optional. Used only during pairwise option comparisons.\"\n                      persistent-hint\n                      counter=\"200\"\n                      maxlength=\"200\"\n                      density=\"compact\"\n                      class=\"mb-2\"\n                    />\n                    <v-checkbox v-model=\"c.disabled\" label=\"Disabled (exclude from comparisons)\" density=\"compact\" hide-details color=\"warning\" />\n                  </div>\n                  <v-btn icon variant=\"text\" @click=\"criteria.splice(idx, 1)\"><i class=\"fa-solid fa-trash\" /></v-btn>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n              <div class=\"d-flex justify-end mt-2\">\n                <v-btn size=\"small\" variant=\"tonal\" @click=\"addFactor\">\n                  <i class=\"fa-solid fa-plus mr-2\" /> Add factor\n                </v-btn>\n              </div>\n            </template>\n          </v-card-text>\n        </v-card>\n      </v-window-item>\n\n      <v-window-item value=\"review\">\n        <v-card class=\"glass-card\">\n          <v-card-text class=\"pa-6\">\n            <div class=\"text-h6 font-weight-bold mb-2\">{{ form.project_title || 'Untitled project' }}</div>\n            <div class=\"text-body-2 text-medium-emphasis mb-4\">{{ form.project_description }}</div>\n            <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap ga-2 mb-4\">\n              <v-chip>{{ mode === 'exclusive' ? 'Pick one' : 'Rank all' }}</v-chip>\n              <v-chip>{{ namedOptions.length }} options</v-chip>\n              <v-chip>{{ factorMode === 'multi' ? namedCriteria.length + ' factors' : 'Overall only' }}</v-chip>\n            </div>\n            <v-alert v-if=\"validationError\" type=\"error\" variant=\"tonal\" class=\"mb-4\">{{ validationError }}</v-alert>\n          </v-card-text>\n        </v-card>\n      </v-window-item>\n    </v-window>\n\n    <div class=\"d-flex justify-space-between mt-5\">\n      <v-btn variant=\"text\" :disabled=\"tab === 'overview'\" @click=\"prevTab\">Back</v-btn>\n      <div class=\"d-flex ga-2\">\n        <template v-if=\"tab !== 'review'\">\n          <v-btn\n            v-if=\"!isNew\"\n            color=\"primary\"\n            variant=\"flat\"\n            :loading=\"saving\"\n            @click=\"save({ stay: true })\"\n          >\n            Update\n          </v-btn>\n          <v-btn color=\"primary\" variant=\"tonal\" @click=\"nextTab\">Continue</v-btn>\n        </template>\n        <template v-else>\n          <v-btn\n            color=\"primary\"\n            variant=\"tonal\"\n            :loading=\"saving\"\n            @click=\"save()\"\n          >\n            {{ isNew ? 'Save' : 'Update' }}\n          </v-btn>\n          <v-btn color=\"primary\" variant=\"flat\" :loading=\"saving\" @click=\"save({ startProbe: true })\">\n            {{ isNew ? 'Save' : 'Update' }} & start comparisons\n          </v-btn>\n        </template>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- Import picker -->\n    <v-dialog v-model=\"importDialog\" max-width=\"560\">\n      <v-card class=\"pc-dialog-card\">\n        <v-card-title class=\"d-flex justify-space-between align-center pa-6 pb-2\">\n          <span class=\"text-h6 font-weight-bold font-display\">{{ importDialogTitle }}</span>\n          <v-btn icon variant=\"text\" size=\"small\" @click=\"importDialog = false\"><i class=\"fa-solid fa-xmark\" /></v-btn>\n        </v-card-title>\n        <v-card-text class=\"px-6 pt-2\">\n          <v-radio-group v-model=\"selectedTemplateId\" :disabled=\"!importCandidates.length\">\n            <v-radio\n              v-for=\"t in importCandidates\"\n              :key=\"t.id\"\n              :value=\"t.id\"\n              :label=\"t.factor_template_title || `Template ${t.id}`\"\n            >\n              <template #label>\n                <div>\n                  <div>{{ t.factor_template_title || `Template ${t.id}` }}</div>\n                  <div class=\"text-caption text-medium-emphasis\">{{ t.factor_template_description || '' }}</div>\n                </div>\n              </template>\n            </v-radio>\n          </v-radio-group>\n          <div v-if=\"!importCandidates.length\" class=\"text-medium-emphasis\">No templates available for this page.</div>\n        </v-card-text>\n        <v-card-actions class=\"px-6 pb-5 pt-0\">\n          <v-spacer />\n          <v-btn variant=\"text\" @click=\"importDialog = false\">Cancel</v-btn>\n          <v-btn\n            color=\"primary\"\n            variant=\"flat\"\n            :disabled=\"!selectedTemplateId\"\n            @click=\"confirmImport\"\n          >\n            {{ importConfirmLabel }}\n          </v-btn>\n        </v-card-actions>\n      </v-card>\n    </v-dialog>\n\n    <!-- Save as template -->\n    <v-dialog v-model=\"saveTemplateDialog\" max-width=\"720\" scrollable>\n      <v-card class=\"pc-dialog-card\">\n        <v-card-title class=\"d-flex justify-space-between align-center pa-6 pb-2\">\n          <span class=\"text-h6 font-weight-bold font-display\">Save as Template</span>\n          <v-btn icon variant=\"text\" size=\"small\" @click=\"saveTemplateDialog = false\"><i class=\"fa-solid fa-xmark\" /></v-btn>\n        </v-card-title>\n        <v-card-text class=\"px-6 pt-2\">\n          <div class=\"template-note mb-4\">\n            <i class=\"fa-solid fa-copy mt-1\" />\n            <div>Creates a copy of the current project settings, options, and factors. Later edits stay independent.</div>\n          </div>\n          <v-text-field v-model=\"saveEditor.title\" label=\"Template name\" variant=\"outlined\" class=\"mb-2\" />\n          <v-textarea v-model=\"saveEditor.description\" label=\"Description\" variant=\"outlined\" rows=\"2\" class=\"mb-3\" />\n          <div class=\"text-subtitle-2 mb-2\">Offer for import on</div>\n          <div class=\"d-flex flex-wrap ga-4 mb-4\">\n            <v-checkbox v-model=\"saveEditor.use_with_projects\" label=\"Project\" density=\"compact\" hide-details color=\"primary\" />\n            <v-checkbox v-model=\"saveEditor.use_with_options\" label=\"Options\" density=\"compact\" hide-details color=\"primary\" />\n            <v-checkbox v-model=\"saveEditor.use_with_factors\" label=\"Factors\" density=\"compact\" hide-details color=\"primary\" />\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"text-body-2 text-medium-emphasis mb-2\">\n            Includes {{ namedOptions.length }} option(s),\n            {{ factorMode === 'multi' ? namedCriteria.length : 0 }} factor(s),\n            mode {{ mode === 'exclusive' ? 'Choose one' : 'Rank all' }},\n            private participation {{ form.private_participation ? 'on' : 'off' }}.\n          </div>\n          <v-switch\n            v-if=\"isSystemAdmin\"\n            v-model=\"saveEditor.enable_global_share\"\n            label=\"Globally shared\"\n            color=\"primary\"\n            hint=\"Visible to all workspaces. System admin only.\"\n            persistent-hint\n          />\n        </v-card-text>\n        <v-card-actions class=\"px-6 pb-5 pt-0\">\n          <v-spacer />\n          <v-btn variant=\"text\" @click=\"saveTemplateDialog = false\">Cancel</v-btn>\n          <v-btn color=\"primary\" variant=\"flat\" :loading=\"savingTemplate\" @click=\"confirmSaveAsTemplate\">Save template</v-btn>\n        </v-card-actions>\n      </v-card>\n    </v-dialog>\n  </div>\n</template>\n\n<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport type { FactorTemplate } from '~/types/api'\nimport { formatEndDateMdY, localEndTimeIso } from '~/utils/projectEndTime'\nimport {\n  DEFAULT_QUESTIONS_PER_GROUP,\n  clampMaxRecommendedPasses,\n  clampMinExpectedPasses,\n  clampQuestionsPerGroup,\n  defaultQuestionsPerGroupForN,\n  questionBudgetBounds,\n} from '~/utils/sortCompare'\n\nconst route = useRoute()\nconst api = useProjectsApi()\nconst brandingApi = useBrandingApi()\nconst snackbar = useSnackbar()\nconst { requireAdmin, isSystemAdmin, isAdmin, aiFeaturesEnabled } = useAuth()\nconst { apiFetch } = useApi()\nconst suggestingFactors = ref(false)\nconst loadingAiModels = ref(false)\nconst aiCatalog = ref<Array<{ id: string, name: string }>>([])\nconst aiCatalogDefault = ref<string | null>(null)\nconst aiCatalogDefaultIsSystem = ref(false)\nconst aiCatalogError = ref('')\nconst DEFAULT_AI_MODEL_KEY = '__default__'\nconst aiVoterItems = computed(() => {\n  const rows = [\n    { title: 'AI AGENT: Default Model', value: DEFAULT_AI_MODEL_KEY },\n    ...aiCatalog.value.map(m => ({ title: `AI AGENT: ${m.name || m.id}`, value: m.id })),\n  ]\n  const seen = new Set<string>()\n  return rows.filter((row) => {\n    if (seen.has(row.value)) return false\n    seen.add(row.value)\n    return true\n  })\n})\nconst aiVoterHint = computed(() => {\n  if (aiCatalogError.value) return aiCatalogError.value\n  if (!aiCatalog.value.length) return 'Configure an AI provider in Settings, or use the system default model.'\n  return 'Select unique models. Default Model uses the workspace or system setting.'\n})\n\nasync function loadAiVoterCatalog() {\n  if (!aiFeaturesEnabled.value) return\n  loadingAiModels.value = true\n  aiCatalogError.value = ''\n  try {\n    const res = await apiFetch<{\n      failure_reason?: string\n      models?: Array<{ id: string, name: string }>\n      default_model?: string | null\n      default_is_system?: boolean\n    }>('/ws/core/llm-available-models')\n    if (res.failure_reason) {\n      aiCatalogError.value = res.failure_reason\n      return\n    }\n    aiCatalog.value = res.models || []\n    aiCatalogDefault.value = res.default_model || null\n    aiCatalogDefaultIsSystem.value = !!res.default_is_system\n    if (form.include_ai_agents && !form.ai_voter_models.length) {\n      form.ai_voter_models = [aiCatalogDefaultIsSystem.value ? DEFAULT_AI_MODEL_KEY : (aiCatalogDefault.value || DEFAULT_AI_MODEL_KEY)]\n    }\n  }\n  catch (e: unknown) {\n    aiCatalogError.value = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Could not load models'\n  }\n  finally {\n    loadingAiModels.value = false\n  }\n}\nconst canSuggestFactors = computed(() =>\n  !!aiFeaturesEnabled.value\n  && !!(form.project_title || '').trim()\n  && !!(form.project_description || '').trim(),\n)\n\nasync function suggestFactorsFromProblem() {\n  if (!requireAdmin() || !canSuggestFactors.value) return\n  const named = namedCriteria.value.filter(c => c.title.trim())\n  if (named.length && !window.confirm('Replace the current unsaved factors with AI suggestions?')) return\n  suggestingFactors.value = true\n  try {\n    const res = await api.suggestFactors({\n      project_id: isNew.value ? 0 : projectId.value,\n      project_title: form.project_title,\n      project_description: form.project_description,\n    })\n    if (res.failure_reason) throw new Error(res.failure_reason)\n    const rows = res.suggestions || []\n    if (!rows.length) throw new Error('No factors were suggested')\n    factorMode.value = 'multi'\n    criteria.value = rows.map(r => ({\n      title: r.title || '',\n      description: r.description || '',\n      comparison_question: '',\n      disabled: false,\n    }))\n    snackbar.success('Suggested factors added. Review, then save.')\n  }\n  catch (e: unknown) {\n    snackbar.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Could not suggest factors')\n  }\n  finally {\n    suggestingFactors.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nconst projectId = computed(() => Number(route.params.id))\nconst isNew = computed(() => !projectId.value || projectId.value <= 0)\n\nconst projectBrandFileInput = ref<HTMLInputElement | null>(null)\nconst projectBrandHasImage = ref(false)\nconst projectBrandingPreview = ref('')\nconst projectBrandUploading = ref(false)\nconst projectBrandDeleting = ref(false)\nconst projectBrandError = ref('')\n\nasync function loadProjectBranding() {\n  if (isNew.value) return\n  projectBrandError.value = ''\n  try {\n    const res = await brandingApi.projectMeta(projectId.value)\n    projectBrandHasImage.value = !!res.has_image\n    projectBrandingPreview.value = res.has_image\n      ? brandingApi.projectImageUrl(projectId.value, res.modify_date || Date.now())\n      : ''\n  }\n  catch {\n    projectBrandHasImage.value = false\n    projectBrandingPreview.value = ''\n  }\n}\n\nasync function onProjectBrandFile(ev: Event) {\n  const input = ev.target as HTMLInputElement\n  const file = input.files?.[0]\n  input.value = ''\n  if (!file || isNew.value) return\n  if (file.size > 8 * 1_048_576) {\n    projectBrandError.value = 'Image must be 8 MB or smaller'\n    return\n  }\n  projectBrandUploading.value = true\n  projectBrandError.value = ''\n  try {\n    const res = await brandingApi.uploadProjectImage(projectId.value, file)\n    if (res.failure_reason) {\n      projectBrandError.value = res.failure_reason\n      return\n    }\n    projectBrandHasImage.value = true\n    projectBrandingPreview.value = brandingApi.projectImageUrl(projectId.value, res.modify_date || Date.now())\n    snackbar.success('Project branding image saved')\n  }\n  catch (e: unknown) {\n    projectBrandError.value = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Upload failed'\n  }\n  finally {\n    projectBrandUploading.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nasync function onDeleteProjectBrand() {\n  if (isNew.value) return\n  projectBrandDeleting.value = true\n  projectBrandError.value = ''\n  try {\n    const res = await brandingApi.deleteProjectImage(projectId.value)\n    if (res.failure_reason) {\n      projectBrandError.value = res.failure_reason\n      return\n    }\n    projectBrandHasImage.value = false\n    projectBrandingPreview.value = ''\n    snackbar.success('Project branding image removed')\n  }\n  catch (e: unknown) {\n    projectBrandError.value = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Delete failed'\n  }\n  finally {\n    projectBrandDeleting.value = false\n  }\n}\nconst duplicateFromId = computed(() => {\n  const raw = route.query.from\n  const n = Number(Array.isArray(raw) ? raw[0] : raw)\n  return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? n : 0\n})\nconst isDuplicateDraft = ref(false)\n\nconst tab = ref('overview')\nconst tabs = ['overview', 'options', 'factors', 'review']\n\nwatch(tab, (val) => {\n  if (val !== 'back-overview') return\n  tab.value = 'overview'\n  goToProjectOverview()\n})\nconst saving = ref(false)\nconst loadingDraft = ref(false)\n\nconst form = reactive({\n  project_title: '',\n  project_tag: '',\n  project_description: '',\n  disabled: false,\n  end_time: null as string | null,\n  private_participation: false,\n  participant_influence_mode: 'comparisons' as 'comparisons' | 'balanced' | 'participants_normalized',\n  participant_influence_min_comparisons: 10,\n  factor_weight_floor_alpha: 0.5,\n  min_expected_passes: 2,\n  max_recommended_passes: 2,\n  include_ai_agents: false,\n  ai_voter_models: [] as string[],\n  option_questions_per_group: DEFAULT_QUESTIONS_PER_GROUP,\n  factor_questions_per_group: DEFAULT_QUESTIONS_PER_GROUP,\n  project_criteria_template: 0,\n})\nconst optionGroupSizeExplicit = ref(false)\nconst factorGroupSizeExplicit = ref(false)\nconst syncingGroupSize = ref(false)\nconst privateParticipationLocked = ref(false)\nconst advancedOpen = ref(false)\nconst endTimeMenu = ref(false)\nconst endTimeMin = computed(() => {\n  const n = new Date()\n  return new Date(n.getFullYear(), n.getMonth(), n.getDate())\n})\nconst endTimeDisplay = computed(() => form.end_time ? formatEndDateMdY(form.end_time) : 'None')\nconst endTimePicker = computed({\n  get() {\n    if (!form.end_time) return null\n    const d = new Date(form.end_time)\n    return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d\n  },\n  set(value: Date | string | null) {\n    applyEndDate(value)\n  },\n})\n\nfunction applyEndDate(value: Date | string | null | undefined) {\n  if (!value) {\n    form.end_time = null\n    return\n  }\n  const d = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value)\n  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return\n  form.end_time = localEndTimeIso(d)\n}\n\nfunction onEndDatePicked(value: Date | string | null) {\n  applyEndDate(value)\n  if (value) endTimeMenu.value = false\n}\n\nfunction clearEndTime() {\n  form.end_time = null\n  endTimeMenu.value = false\n}\nconst mode = ref<'exclusive' | 'rank'>('exclusive')\nconst rankingMode = ref<'find_best' | 'find_top_3' | 'find_top_half' | 'rank_all'>('rank_all')\nconst rankingModeHint = computed(() => {\n  if (rankingMode.value === 'find_best') return 'Concentrate comparisons on identifying the single best option.'\n  if (rankingMode.value === 'find_top_3') return 'Concentrate comparisons around the top-three boundary. Available with six or more options.'\n  if (rankingMode.value === 'find_top_half') return 'Concentrate comparisons on separating the top half of options.'\n  return 'Spend the question budget on a full ranking from best to worst.'\n})\nwatch(rankingMode, (v) => {\n  mode.value = v === 'find_best' ? 'exclusive' : 'rank'\n})\nconst factorMode = ref<'overall' | 'multi'>('overall')\n\nfunction clampFactorWeightFloorAlpha(value: unknown): number {\n  const n = Number(value)\n  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0.5\n  return Math.min(0.9, Math.max(0.1, Math.round(n * 20) / 20))\n}\n\nconst maxPassesFloor = computed(() => clampMinExpectedPasses(form.min_expected_passes))\n\nwatch(() => form.min_expected_passes, (v) => {\n  const mn = clampMinExpectedPasses(v)\n  if (form.max_recommended_passes < mn) form.max_recommended_passes = mn\n})\n\nconst privateParticipationSwitchDisabled = computed(\n  () => privateParticipationLocked.value && !isSystemAdmin.value,\n)\nconst privateParticipationHint = computed(() => {\n  const base = 'When on, reports show unique participant 1, 2, … instead of names. Locked once comparisons are collected.'\n  if (!privateParticipationLocked.value) return base\n  if (isSystemAdmin.value) {\n    return `${base} System Admin - This can be reversed`\n  }\n  return `${base} Locked.`\n})\nconst influenceModeHint = computed(() => {\n  if (form.participant_influence_mode === 'balanced') {\n    return 'Moderately reduces the pull of participants who made many more comparisons.'\n  }\n  if (form.participant_influence_mode === 'participants_normalized') {\n    return 'Strongly normalizes influence across participants (not fully equal). Best when contribution volume varies widely.'\n  }\n  return 'Every comparison counts the same (default). Heavy contributors have more total influence.'\n})\n\ntype ContentRow = {\n  id?: number\n  title: string\n  description: string\n  comparison_question?: string\n  disabled: boolean\n}\n\nconst options = ref<ContentRow[]>([\n  { title: '', description: '', disabled: false },\n  { title: '', description: '', disabled: false },\n])\nconst criteria = ref<ContentRow[]>([{ title: '', description: '', comparison_question: '', disabled: false }])\nconst existingTag = ref('')\n\n// Import dialog\ntype ImportScope = 'projects' | 'options' | 'factors'\nconst importDialog = ref(false)\nconst importScope = ref<ImportScope>('factors')\nconst importCandidates = ref<FactorTemplate[]>([])\nconst selectedTemplateId = ref<number | null>(null)\nconst allTemplates = ref<FactorTemplate[]>([])\n\nconst importDialogTitle = computed(() => {\n  if (importScope.value === 'projects') return 'Start with Template'\n  if (importScope.value === 'options') return 'Import Options from Template'\n  return 'Import Factors from Template'\n})\nconst importConfirmLabel = computed(() => {\n  if (importScope.value === 'projects') return 'Import'\n  if (importScope.value === 'options') return 'Import Options'\n  return 'Import Factors'\n})\n\n// Save as template\nconst saveTemplateDialog = ref(false)\nconst savingTemplate = ref(false)\nconst saveEditor = reactive({\n  title: '',\n  description: '',\n  use_with_projects: true,\n  use_with_options: true,\n  use_with_factors: true,\n  enable_global_share: false,\n})\n\nfunction addOption() {\n  options.value.push({ [] : (await api.listAlternatives(projectId)).alternative_info_list || []\n  const byId = new Map(currentAlts.map(a => [a.id, a]))\n  const keepIds = new Set<number>()\n\n  for (const opt of namedOptions.value) {\n    const title = opt.title.trim()\n    const description = opt.description.trim()\n    const disabled = !!opt.disabled\n    if (opt.id && byId.has(opt.id)) {\n      keepIds.add(opt.id)\n      const existing = byId.get(opt.id)!\n      const same\n        = (existing.alternative_title || '') === title\n          && (existing.alternative_description || '') === description\n          && !!existing.disabled === disabled\n      if (!same) {\n        const res = await api.updateAlternative({\n          alternative_id: opt.id,\n          project_id: projectId,\n          alternative_title: title,\n          alternative_description: description,\n          disabled,\n        })\n        if (res.failure_reason) throw new Error(res.failure_reason)\n      }\n    }\n    else {\n      const res = await api.createAlternative({\n        project_id: projectId,\n        alternative_title: title,\n        alternative_description: description,\n        disabled,\n      })\n      if (res.failure_reason || !res.alternative_info) throw new Error(res.failure_reason || 'Create option failed')\n      opt.id = res.alternative_info.id\n      keepIds.add(res.alternative_info.id)\n    }\n  }\n\n  for (const a of currentAlts) {\n    if (!keepIds.has(a.id)) await retireAlternative(projectId, a.id)\n  }\n}\n\nasync function syncFactors(projectId: number) {\n  const currentFacts = isNew.value ? \n341: function primaryAction(row: ProjectRow) {\n342:   const id = row.project.id\n343:   if (row.altCount < 2) {\n344:     return { label: 'Continue setup', to: `/projects/${id}/edit`, color: 'primary', variant: 'tonal' as const }\n345:   }\n346:   if (row.project.disabled || collectionIsClosed(row.project.end_time)) {\n347:     return { label: 'View results', to: `/projects/${id}/results`, color: 'primary', variant: 'flat' as const }\n348:   }\n349:   if (row.obsCount === 0) {\n350:     return { label: 'Start comparing', to: `/projects/${id}/probe`, color: 'primary', variant: 'flat' as const }\n351:   }\n352:   if (row.metrics.completion >= 80) {\n353:     return { label: 'View results', to: `/projects/${id}/results`, color: 'primary', variant: 'flat' ProjectRow) {\n359:   const id = row.project.id\n360:   const items: Array<{ title: string, className?: string, run: () => void }> = [\n361:     { title: 'Open project', run: () => navigateTo(`/projects/${id}`) },\n362:     { title: 'Edit Settings', run: () => navigateTo(`/projects/${id}/edit`) },\n363:     { title: 'Make a copy', run: () => makeDuplicate(id) },\n364:     { title: 'Invite people', run: () => navigateTo(`/projects/${id}/shares?invite=1`) },\n365:   ]\n366:   if (row.altCount >= 2 && !row.project.disabled && !collectionIsClosed(row.project.end_time)) {\n367:     items.push({ title: 'Start comparing', run: () => navigateTo(`/projects/${id}/probe`) })\n368:   }\n369:   items.push(\n370:     { title: 'View results', run: () => navigateTo(`/projects/${id}/results`) },\n371:     { title: 'Delete', className: 'text-error', run: () => confirmDelete(row.project) },\n372:   )\n373:   return items\n374: }\n375: \n376: async function load() {\n377:   loading.value = true\n378:   try {\n379:     const result = await api.listProjectsSummary()\n380:     if (result.failure_reason) throw new Error(result.failure_reason)\n381:     rows.value = (result.projects || []).map(item => ({\n382:       project: item.project,\n383:       altCount: item.alternative_count || 0,\n384:       factorCount: item.factor_count || 0,\n385:       obsCount: item.observation_count || 0,\n386:       participantCount: item.participant_count || 0,\n387:       metrics: {\n388:         completion: asMetricPercent(item.metrics?.completion ?? catch (e: unknown) {\n395:     snackbar.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to load projects')\n396:   }\n397:   finally {\n398:     loading.value = false\n399:   }\n400: }\n401: \n402: function makeDuplicate(projectId: number) {\n403:   if (!requireAdmin()) return\n404:   navigateTo(`/projects/0/edit?from=${projectId}`)\n405: }\n406: \n407: function confirmDelete(project: `/projects/${id}/results`, color: 'primary', variant: 'flat' as const }\n  }\n  return { label: 'View preliminary results', to: `/projects/${id}/results`, color: 'primary', variant: 'flat' as const }\n}\n\nfunction projectMenuItems(row: ProjectRow) {\n  const id = row.project.id\n  const items: Array<{ title: string, className?: string, run: () => void }> = [\n    { title: 'Open project', run: () => navigateTo(`/projects/${id}`) },\n    { title: 'Edit Settings', run: () => navigateTo(`/projects/${id}/edit`) },\n    { title: 'Make a copy', run: () => makeDuplicate(id) },\n    { title: 'Invite people', run: () => navigateTo(`/projects/${id}/shares?invite=1`) },\n  ]\n  if (row.altCount >= 2 && !row.project.disabled && !collectionIsClosed(row.project.end_time)) {\n    items.push({ title: 'Start comparing', run: () => navigateTo(`/projects/${id}/probe`) })\n  }\n  items.push(\n    { title: 'View results', run: () => navigateTo(`/projects/${id}/results`) },\n    { title: 'Delete', className: 'text-error', run: () => confirmDelete(row.project) },\n  )\n  return items\n}\n\nasync function load() {\n  loading.value = true\n  try {\n    const result = await api.listProjectsSummary()\n    if (result.failure_reason) throw new Error(result.failure_reason)\n    rows.value = (result.projects || []).map(item => ({\n      project: item.project,\n      altCount: item.alternative_count || 0,\n      factorCount: item.factor_count || 0,\n      obsCount: item.observation_count || 0,\n      participantCount: item.participant_count || 0,\n      metrics: {\n        completion: asMetricPercent(item.metrics?.completion ?? 0),\n        stability: asMetricPercent(item.metrics?.stability ?? 0),\n        confidence: asMetricPercent(item.metrics?.confidence ?? 0),\n      },\n    }))\n  }\n  catch (e: unknown) {\n    snackbar.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to load projects')\n  }\n  finally {\n    loading.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nfunction makeDuplicate(projectId: number) {\n  if (!requireAdmin()) return\n  navigateTo(`/projects/0/edit?from=${projectId}`)\n}\n\nfunction confirmDelete(project: CustomerProject) {\n  if (!requireAdmin()) return\n  pendingDelete.value = project\n  deleteDialog.value = true\n}\n\nasync function doDelete() {\n  if (!pendingDelete.value) return\n  deleting.value = true\n  try {\n    const res = await api.deleteProject(pendingDelete.value.id, pendingDelete.value.project_tag)\n    if (res.failure_reason) throw new Error(res.failure_reason)\n    snackbar.success('Project deleted')\n    deleteDialog.value = false\n    await load()\n  }\n  catch (e: unknown) {\n    snackbar.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Delete failed')\n  }\n  finally {\n    deleting.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nonMounted(load)\n</script>","lineStart":340,"lineEnd":432,"totalLines":432,"truncated":false}},"title":"client/pages/projects/index.vue","time":{"start":1787323191028,"end":1787323191400}},"id":"prt_024c38ed5001a4g75f7Q9oiPyM","sessionID":"ses_fdb3da858ffeJLw3G6vr3RanSM","messageID":"msg_024c369c2001K4DjXS6p5pSyZy"}}

e.message : 'Failed to load sharing'\n496:     if (String(msg).includes('No matching')) {\n497:       sharesByProject.value = {}\n498:     }\n499:     else {\n500:       snackbar.error(msg)\n501:     }\n502:   }\n503:   finally {\n504:     loading.value = false\n505:   }\n506: }\n507: \n508: onMounted(load)\n509: </script>\n\n(End of file - total 509 lines)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"\nfunction openDetail(entry: ShareLinkAuthsAccesses, row: ProjectShareRow) {\n  detailEntry.value = entry\n  detailProjectTitle.value = row.title\n  detailOpen.value = true\n}\n\nfunction copyLink(url: string) {\n  if (url) copyText(url, 'Share link')\n}\n\nfunction canSendInvite(entry: ShareLinkAuthsAccesses | null) {\n  const link = entry?.share_link\n  if (!link?.shared_with_email) return false\n  if (!link.share_link_enabled) return false\n  if (entry?.share_link_expired) return false\n  return true\n}\n\nasync function sendOrResendInvite(entry: ShareLinkAuthsAccesses) {","truncated":false,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/client/pages/shares/index.vue","text":"\nfunction openDetail(entry: ShareLinkAuthsAccesses, row: ProjectShareRow) {\n  detailEntry.value = entry\n  detailProjectTitle.value = row.title\n  detailOpen.value = true\n}\n\nfunction copyLink(url: string) {\n  if (url) copyText(url, 'Share link')\n}\n\nfunction canSendInvite(entry: ShareLinkAuthsAccesses | null) {\n  const link = entry?.share_link\n  if (!link?.shared_with_email) return false\n  if (!link.share_link_enabled) return false\n  if (entry?.share_link_expired) return false\n  return true\n}\n\nasync function sendOrResendInvite(entry: ShareLinkAuthsAccesses) {\n  if (!canSendInvite(entry) || !requireAdmin()) return\n  sendingInviteId.value = entry.share_id\n  try {\n    const res = await sharesApi.sendInvitation({ share_id: entry.share_id })\n    if (res.failure_reason) throw new Error(res.failure_reason)\n    snackbar.success(res.was_resend ? 'Invitation resent' : 'Invitation sent')\n    entry.invite_email_sent = true\n    await load()\n    const refreshed = (sharesByProject.value[entry.project_id] || []).find(s => s.share_id === entry.share_id)\n    if (refreshed) detailEntry.value = refreshed\n  }\n  catch (e: unknown) {\n    snackbar.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Could not send invitation')\n  }\n  finally {\n    sendingInviteId.value = null\n  }\n}\n\nasync function load() {\n  loading.value = true\n  try {\n    const result = await projectsApi.listProjectsSummary()\n    if (result.failure_reason) throw new Error(result.failure_reason)\n    const map: Record<number, CustomerProject> = {}\n    const ids: number[] = []\n    for (const item of result.projects || []) {\n      if (item.project?.id != null) {\n        map[item.project.id] = item.project\n        ids.push(item.project.id)\n      }\n    }\n    projectMap.value = map\n\n    const activity = await sharesApi.activityForProjects(ids.length ? ids : [-1])\n    const byProject: Record<number, ShareLinkAuthsAccesses[]> = {}\n    if (!activity.failure_reason || activity.share_info_list_per_project_id) {\n      const raw = activity.share_info_list_per_project_id || {}\n      for (const [key, list] of Object.entries(raw)) {\n        byProject[Number(key)] = list || []\n      }\n    }\n    sharesByProject.value = byProject\n  }\n  catch (e: unknown) {\n    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to load sharing'\n    if (String(msg).includes('No matching')) {\n      sharesByProject.value = {}\n    }\n    else {\n      snackbar.error(msg)\n    }\n  }\n  finally {\n    loading.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nonMounted(load)\n</script>","lineStart":430,"lineEnd":509,"totalLines":509,"truncated":false}},"title":"client/pages/shares/index.vue","time":{"start":1787323202520,"end":1787323203869}},"id":"prt_024c3bb83001bq53VwtA8bCwId","sessionID":"ses_fdb3da858ffeJLw3G6vr3RanSM","messageID":"msg_024c399f5001oDkmUUVSCVG9ZH"}}

{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787323490251,"sessionID":"ses_fdb3da858ffeJLw3G6vr3RanSM","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"edit","callID":"call_53285a31ee7249c2828e4584","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/client/pages/projects/[id]/edit.vue","newString":"async function loadDuplicateDraft() {\n  if (!isNew.value || !duplicateFromId.value) return\n  await applyDuplicateSource(duplicateFromId.value)\n}\n\n// Copy from project\nconst copyFromDialog = ref(false)\nconst copyFromCandidates = ref<ProjectListSummaryItem[]>([])\nconst copyFromSelectedId = ref<number | null>(null)\nconst copyFromSearch = ref('')\nconst canCopyFromProject = computed(() => !namedOptions.value.length && !namedCriteria.value.length)\nconst copyFromDisabledHint = computed(() => (canCopyFromProject.value ? undefined : 'Clear entered options before copying'))\nconst copyFromFiltered = computed(() => {\n  const q = copyFromSearch.value.trim().toLowerCase()\n  if (!q) return copyFromCandidates.value\n  return copyFromCandidates.value.filter(item =>\n    (item.project.project_title || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)\n    || (item.project.project_tag || '').toLowerCase().includes(q),\n  )\n})\n\nasync function openCopyFromDialog() {\n  if (!isNew.value || !canCopyFromProject.value) return\n  loadingDraft.value = true\n  try {\n    const res = await api.listProjectsSummary()\n    if (res.failure_reason) throw new Error(res.failure_reason)\n    copyFromCandidates.value = res.projects || []\n    copyFromSelectedId.value = null\n    copyFromSearch.value = ''\n    copyFromDialog.value = true\n  }\n  catch (e: unknown) {\n    snackbar.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Could not load projects')\n  }\n  finally {\n    loadingDraft.value = false\n  }\n}\n\nasync function confirmCopyFromProject() {\n  const item = copyFromCandidates.value.find(x => x.project?.id === copyFromSelectedId.value)\n  if (!item?.project?.id) return\n  const sourceName = item.project.project_title || item.project.project_tag || `project ${item.project.id}`\n  copyFromDialog.value = false\n  try {\n    await applyDuplicateSource(item.project.id)\n    snackbar.success(`Settings copied from ${sourceName}`)\n  }\n  catch (e: unknown) {\n    snackbar.error(e instanceof Error ? {"type":"text","timestamp":1787323741150,"sessionID":"ses_fdb3da858ffeJLw3G6vr3RanSM","part":{"id":"prt_024cbc06f001zOaZIr1BlAhaAq","messageID":"msg_024cbb47f001ib4zXBc8wvKkgJ","sessionID":"ses_fdb3da858ffeJLw3G6vr3RanSM","type":"text","text":"## Build Summary\n\n**\"Copy from project\" on the project create page — implemented, verified, committed.**\n\n### What was built\n- **Button**: `Copy from project` (small/tonal) rendered only in new mode, immediately left of `Start with Template`; disabled with tooltip once any option or factor is named in the draft.\n- **Picker dialog**: searchable radio list of account projects (`listProjectsSummary`) showing title, tag, and option/factor counts; empty states for no projects / no search matches; Cancel/**Copy** gated on selection.\n- **Copy behavior**: exact Duplicate Project parity via the shared duplicate loader — settings + options/factors copied; votes, shares, end time, disabled flag, branding, and template linkage excluded; title/tag get `- Copy`/slug treatment; header switches to \"New project (copy)\".\n- **Refactor**: `loadDuplicateDraft()` body extracted into shared `applyDuplicateSource(sourceProjectId)` so the `?from=` deep-link and the dialog use byte-identical logic.\n\n### Verification\n- `./scripts/nuxt/update_static_client.sh` completed successfully (production generate + publish to `static_client/`); no SFC/build errors. No backend/OpenAPI changes → no pytest or snapshot work per plan.\n- One deliberate deviation from plan wording: used `fa-copy` icon instead of a second `fa-clone`, since `Start with Template` directly beside it already uses `fa-clone` (identical adjacent icons would read as duplicates).\n\n### Commit\n- **Hash/message**: `5e9f451` — \"Add \\\"Copy from project\\\" to the project create page\"\n- Staged only `client/pages/projects/[id]/edit.vue` + `src/config/config_settings.py` (version bump 0.7.69 per build policy; note this file also carried a prior uncommitted 0.7.67→0.7.68 bump now subsumed). Unrelated dirty files left untouched. No CHANGELOG.md exists in-repo, so no changelog note.\n\n### Major touch points (`client/pages/projects/[id]/edit.vue`)\n| Anchor | Location |\n|---|---|\n| Button (disabled rule + tooltip binding) | edit.vue:38–47 |\n| Picker dialog (search, list, counts, empty states) | edit.vue:565–610 |\n| `ProjectListSummaryItem` type import | edit.vue:655 |\n| Shared `applyDuplicateSource()` helper | edit.vue:1363–1376 |\n| `loadDuplicateDraft()` now delegates to helper | edit.vue:1378–1381 |\n| State + `canCopyFromProject` / hint / filter computeds | edit.vue:1384–1397 |\n| `openCopyFromDialog()` | edit.vue:1399–1415 |\n| `confirmCopyFromProject()` | edit.vue:1418–1429 |\n| `.copy-from-list` scroll style | edit.vue:1662–1665 |\n| Version bump | src/config/config_settings.py:13 |","time":{"start":1787323727983,"end":1787323741108}}}
